# CREATE3 release salt input inventory

Source baseline: V1-validated working tree snapshotted at `/private/tmp/create3-salt-baseline-3k4ijum3`. FullSpread evidence is read-only. Implementation verification is recorded in [validation](create3-release-salt-input-validation.md).

## Call-path policy

`deployFacet` / `deployPackageWithArgs` / canonical package helpers reach Crane CREATE3; `IVaultRegistryDeployment.deployPkg(code,args,salt)` reaches the manager's existing authorized CREATE3 factory and registers the returned package. Constructor bytes and code stay in the deployment payload. Typed `deployPkg(PkgInit)` routes through the existing component service; its argument is not a salt. Automatic library links use the same factory. No proxy `deploy`, `calcSalt`, `processArgs`, initialization, or CREATE2 algorithm changes.

Baseline search found 626 direct/wrapped call records; 113 shared-helper invocations in 25 files (108/20 contracts, 5/5 scripts). These are discovery counts, not completion criteria. Current expressions below are baseline expressions; replacement expressions are the final source expressions. `salt` parameters on low-level script wrappers are resolved by the identity/caller tables below.

## Exclusions and preservation

- All six Phase 02 `CREATE3_FACTORY_PACKAGE_SALT`, `CALL_TARGET_REGISTRY_PACKAGE_SALT`, `BOUNTY_BOARD_PACKAGE_SALT` constants retain `keccak256(abi.encode(contractIdentifier))` and their call sites (D14); canonical helper salt is its third argument, before interfaceId.
- Main/testnet Phase 02-01 bootstrap uses actual CREATE2 through `InitDevService.initFactory`; its owner/namespace key remains unchanged. `UniswapV4TwapAdapterFactory` uses actual CREATE2 for adapters; onchain bytecode and init hashes stay unchanged. Hook flag mining and callback proxy formulas remain unchanged.
- `deployToken` / optionalSalt keys in Mag7/CoreTestTokens, foundation assets and test-token scripts remain proxy inputs. Pons Script 04 uses CREATE2. Stage constructor/runtime fingerprints remain verification-only.
- D11 composed pool adapters retain their pool keys in `TestBase_FundedComposedDETF`, `TestBase_ComposedStableCommonDetf_Decimals`, integrated composition tests, and Balancer adversarial/decimal tests. FullSpread TwapPoke retains `V2.revertingAdvisoryOracle`; router TestBase retains `IndexedexBalancerV3WeightedPoolFactory`. Direct ERC721 test labels and buffer-pool facet name encodings are unchanged.
- Archive Script 07 imports `./DeploymentBase.sol` at line 4; that file is absent in both baseline and current archive. Its ERC20PermitDFPkg constructor remains `abi.encode(IERC20PermitDFPkg.PkgInit(...))`; its component salt is now `abi.encode("ERC20PermitDFPkg")._hash()`. No prediction expression exists there; token proxy optionalSalt is unchanged. Source-validated, compilation blocked under D7; no compile or runtime success claimed.
- Extra contrary fixture: `RebasingAwareERC4626_Packaging.test_wrongFacetPkgCannotDeployVault` uses direct authorized registry deployment under `abi.encode("RebasingAwareERC4626_Packaging.wrongFacet")._hash()` to retain its original rejection assertion. The DETF wrong-dependency fixture analogously uses the explicit key in the plan; both are D11 corrections needed to preserve negative coverage.

Additional traced fixture: `test/foundry/spec/protocols/staking/rebasingVault/RebasingAwareERC4626_LaunchCompatibility.t.sol:test_PKG05_mismatchedRegistryCannotDeployVault` requires a second real wrong-registry package. It now calls authorized `IVaultRegistryDeployment.deployPkg` directly with `abi.encode("RebasingAwareERC4626_LaunchCompatibility.wrongRegistry")._hash()` (D11), retaining the original deployment rejection assertion. V3 focused root added; V5 also covers it.

### Full-gate follow-up: Stata occupied identity

The full hermetic gate discovered a contrary address-isolation assertion in `AaveStataNativeSY.t.sol`. AC25/D6 now uses `AaveStataOccupiedNamespaceTest` on `CraneTest`, so the real ERC20Facet payload occupies the canonical Stata facet slot before the production helper runs. It asserts the exact CREATE3 prediction, reused address, unchanged runtime and original `ERC20Facet` identity. Stata lifecycle assertions are unchanged; the entire source is included in the focused gate.

## Verification closure

All applicable inventory gates passed: V3 covers 122 tests in 15 suites; V4 prepares runtime artifacts and compiles all 173 maintained roots; V5 passes 32199 tests across 2774 suites. Crane D13 compilation passed. Archived D7 entries retain their explicit source-only exception. See [validation](create3-release-salt-input-validation.md) for commands, logs, vectors, source preservation and final revisions.
